	public static bool IsFastForwardingTime()
	{
		if (!fastForwardTimeToDawn)
		{
			return fastForwardTimeToDusk;
		}
		return true;
	}

	public static void Sundialing()
	{
		if (sundialCooldown == 0)
		{
			if (netMode == 1)
			{
				NetMessage.SendData(51, -1, -1, null, myPlayer, 3f);
				return;
			}
			fastForwardTimeToDawn = true;
			sundialCooldown = 8;
			NetMessage.SendData(7);
		}
	}

	{
		if (moondialCooldown == 0)

			if (netMode == 1)
			{
				NetMessage.SendData(51, -1, -1, null, myPlayer, 6f);
				return;
			}
			fastForwardTimeToDusk = true;
			moondialCooldown = 8;
			NetMessage.SendData(7);
		}
	}

	{
		if (IsFastForwardingTime())

			dayRate = 60;
			desiredWorldTilesUpdateRate = 1;
			return;
		}
		bool enabled = CreativePowerManager.Instance.GetPower<CreativePowers.FreezeTime>().Enabled;
		int targetTimeRate = CreativePowerManager.Instance.GetPower<CreativePowers.ModifyTimeRate>().TargetTimeRate;
		bool flag = CurrentFrameFlags.SleepingPlayersCount == CurrentFrameFlags.ActivePlayersCount && CurrentFrameFlags.SleepingPlayersCount > 0;
		int num = targetTimeRate;
		if (!gameMenu && flag)
		{
			num *= 5;
		}
		if (enabled)
		{
			num = 0;
		}
		dayRate = num;
		desiredWorldTilesUpdateRate = num;
		if (gameMenu)
		{
			dayRate = 1;
			desiredWorldTilesUpdateRate = 1;
		}
	}

	private static void HandleMeteorFall()
	{
		if (netMode != 1 && WorldGen.spawnMeteor)
		{
			if (time < 15000.0)
			{
				_canShowMeteorFall = true;
			}
			else
			{
				if (_canShowMeteorFall && time <= 16200.0)
				{
					AmbienceServer.ForceEntitySpawn(new AmbienceServer.AmbienceSpawnInfo
					{
						skyEntityType = SkyEntityType.Meteor,
						targetPlayer = -1
					});
				}
				_canShowMeteorFall = false;
			}
		}
		if (time > 16200.0 && WorldGen.spawnMeteor)
		{
			WorldGen.spawnMeteor = false;
			if (WorldGen.Skyblock.lowTiles)
			{
				WorldGen.StartMeteorShower();
			}
			else if (!WorldGen.dropMeteor() && rand.Next(3) == 0)
			{
				WorldGen.StartMeteorShower();
			}
		}
	}

	private static void UpdateSlimeRainWarning()
	{
		if (slimeWarningTime <= 0)
		{
			return;
		}
		slimeWarningTime--;
		if (slimeWarningTime <= 0)
		{
			if (slimeRainTime > 0.0)
			{
				ChatHelper.BroadcastChatMessage(NetworkText.FromKey(Lang.gen[74].Key), ChatColors.World);
			}
			else
			{
				ChatHelper.BroadcastChatMessage(NetworkText.FromKey(Lang.gen[75].Key), ChatColors.World);
			}
		}
	}

28	  static readonly DAWN = 4.5 / 24;   // 0.1875
29	  static readonly DUSK = 19.5 / 24;  // 0.8125
30	  timeOfDay: number = 8.25 / 24;   // 0-1，0=午夜 0.5=正午（起始 8:15AM = Main.time 13500）

37	   *  hardMode && downedMechBossAny && 1/20）/ 日耀碑牌召唤（Player.cs:43308，白天），

43	  halloween: boolean = false;
44	  readonly dayLengthMs: number;
45	  constructor(dayLengthMs: number) { this.dayLengthMs = dayLengthMs; }
46	  tick(dtMs: number) {
47	    // 原版 Main.time 恒速 tick（1t/帧）：dayLengthMs=全周期 24min 时，0.625/0.375 的
48	    // 昼夜窗口自然得出 15min/9min——★勿再按段调速（曾除反导致昼夜缩水，探针抓回）
49	    this.timeOfDay += dtMs / this.dayLengthMs;
50	    while (this.timeOfDay >= 1) { this.timeOfDay -= 1; this.dayCount++; }
51	  }
52	  get isDay(): boolean { return this.timeOfDay > Clock.DAWN && this.timeOfDay < Clock.DUSK; }
53	  get dayFactor(): number {
54	    // 昼夜平滑系数：白天 1，夜晚 0.32（月光下地表仍可见，火把不再像贴在黑幕上），晨昏过渡
55	    const t = this.timeOfDay;
56	    if (t > Clock.DAWN + 0.04 && t < Clock.DUSK - 0.04) return 1;
57	    if (t >= Clock.DUSK - 0.04 && t < Clock.DUSK + 0.04) return 1 - (t - (Clock.DUSK - 0.04)) / 0.08 * 0.68;
58	    if (t >= Clock.DUSK + 0.04 || t < Clock.DAWN - 0.04) return 0.32;
59	    return 0.32 + (t - (Clock.DAWN - 0.04)) / 0.08 * 0.68;
60	  }

12	class ByteWriter {

17	    let cap = this.buf.length;
18	    while (cap < this.len + need) cap *= 2;
19	    const nb = new Uint8Array(cap);

28	      v >>>= 7;

37	export function bytesToB64(u8: Uint8Array): string {

152	}

155	  name: string; seed: number; w: number; h: number;
156	  spawnX: number; spawnY: number; groundLevel: number; rockLevel: number; lavaLine?: number;
157	  treeX?: number[]; treeStyle?: number[]; treeTops?: number[];
158	  crimson?: boolean; dungeonX?: number; dungeonY?: number; jungleX?: number;
159	  timeOfDay: number; dayCount: number;

163	  difficulty?: number;

165	  journeyTimeRate?: number;
166	  journeyFreezeTime?: boolean;
167	  chests: SaveData['chests']; signs?: SaveData['signs']; furniture?: FurnitureSaveEntry[]; flags: Record<string, boolean>;
168	  weather?: WeatherSave;
169	  invasion?: { type: number; size: number; sizeStart: number; x: number; delay: number };
170	  /** 已砸祭坛数（WorldGen.altarCount，WorldFile.cs:1303/2100；旧档缺省 0） */
171	  altarCount?: number;

173	  bestiary?: import('../data/Bestiary').BestiarySave;
174	}
175	
176	export function rlePairs(values: ArrayLike<number>): Uint8Array {
177	  const out = new ByteWriter();
178	  const n = values.length;
179	  let i = 0;
180	  while (i < n) {
181	    const v = values[i];
182	    let run = 1;
183	    while (i + run < n && values[i + run] === v && run < 0x7fffffff) run++;
184	    out.varint(run - 1);
185	    out.varint(v);
186	    i += run;
187	  }
188	  return out.bytes();
189	}
190	
191	/** RLE tiles。stableMap 非空时逐元素映射为稳定 id(v3 存档路径) */
192	export function rleTiles(st: { type: Uint16Array; frameX: Uint16Array; frameY: Uint16Array }, stableMap?: Uint16Array): Uint8Array {
193	  const out = new ByteWriter();
194	  const n = st.type.length;
195	  let i = 0;
196	  while (i < n) {
197	    const raw = st.type[i];
198	    const t = stableMap ? (raw === 0 ? 0 : stableMap[raw]) : raw;
199	    const fx = st.frameX[i], fy = st.frameY[i];
200	    let run = 1;
201	    // 游程比较必须 raw 对 raw（t 是映射后的稳定 id，与原始 id 比较恒不等 →
202	    // 自定义 tile（stable≠internal）游程全断，4200x1200 规则地形 17B 膨胀到 21MB）
203	    while (i + run < n && st.type[i + run] === raw && st.frameX[i + run] === fx && st.frameY[i + run] === fy) run++;
204	    out.varint(run - 1);
205	    out.varint(t);
206	    if (t !== 0) {
207	      out.varint(fx);
208	      out.varint(fy);
209	    }
210	    i += run;
211	  }
212	  return out.bytes();
213	}
214	
215	/** 存档序列化主体。幽灵净化在视图上就地执行(主线程=活存储原语义;
216	 * worker=副本,净化不影响游戏内状态,存档字节一致) */
217	export function serializeSave(
218	  st: SaveStoreView, meta: SaveMeta,
219	  player: SaveData['player'], playTimeMs: number,
220	  townNpcs?: SaveData['npcs'],
221	  events?: SaveData['events'],
222	): string {
223	  // 幽灵净化:存档格式不携带 active 位(加载时 type≠0 即激活),
224	  // 非活性格上的残留 type 必须归零,否则重读后全部变成浮空实体块
225	  for (let i = 0; i < st.type.length; i++) {
226	    if (!st.flags[i] && st.type[i] !== 0) st.type[i] = 0;
227	  }
228	  // 物品 id → 稳定 id(全槽位统一;量小直接遍历)。保留 favorited/prefix。
229	  // v4 起 armor/dye/trash/miscEquips/miscDyes/loadouts 也走稳定 id(v3 曾裸透传内部
230	  // 下标——注册表一变即错位;本地物品退役时顺手根治)
231	  const mapItem = (it: { id: number; stack: number; favorited?: boolean; prefix?: number } | null) =>
232	    it
233	      ? {
234	        id: it.id === 0 ? 0 : ITEM_STABLE_OF_INTERNAL[it.id] ?? 0,
235	        stack: it.stack,
236	        favorited: it.favorited || undefined,
237	        prefix: it.prefix || undefined,
238	      }
239	      : null;
240	  const data: SaveData = {
241	    format: 'sandboxworld.save',
242	    version: 4,
243	    header: {
244	      name: meta.name, seed: meta.seed, width: meta.w, height: meta.h,
245	      spawn: [meta.spawnX, meta.spawnY], groundLevel: meta.groundLevel, rockLevel: meta.rockLevel,
246	      lavaLine: meta.lavaLine,
247	      treeX: meta.treeX, treeStyle: meta.treeStyle, treeTops: meta.treeTops,
248	      crimson: meta.crimson, dungeonX: meta.dungeonX, dungeonY: meta.dungeonY, jungleX: meta.jungleX,
249	      created: Date.now(), playTimeMs,
250	      moonType: meta.moonType ?? 0,
251	      seedFlags: meta.seedFlags,
252	      difficulty: meta.difficulty ?? 0,   // Main.GameMode（旧档缺省=经典）
253	      journeyTimeRate: meta.journeyTimeRate ?? 1,
254	      journeyFreezeTime: meta.journeyFreezeTime ?? false,
255	    },
256	    clock: {
257	      timeOfDay: meta.timeOfDay, dayCount: meta.dayCount,
258	      bloodMoon: meta.bloodMoon ?? false, eclipse: meta.eclipse ?? false,
259	      moonPhase: meta.moonPhase ?? 0,
260	    },
261	    invasion: meta.invasion ?? { type: 0, size: 0, sizeStart: 0, x: 0, delay: 0 },
262	    altarCount: meta.altarCount ?? 0,
263	    tiles: bytesToB64(rleTiles(st, TILE_STABLE_OF_INTERNAL)),
264	    walls: bytesToB64(rlePairs(st.wall)),
265	    liquid: bytesToB64(rlePairs(st.liquid)),
266	    liquidType: bytesToB64(rlePairs(st.liquidType)),
267	    wire: bytesToB64(rlePairs(st.wire)),
268	    blocks: bytesToB64(rlePairs(combineBlocks(st.half, st.slope))),
269	    chests: meta.chests.map((c) => ({ x: c.x, y: c.y, locked: c.locked || undefined, items: c.items.map(mapItem) })),
270	    // v4:armor/dye/trash/miscEquips/miscDyes/loadouts 逐槽稳定 id(v3 是裸内部下标)
271	    player: {
272	      ...player,
273	      inventory: player.inventory.map(mapItem),
274	      banks: player.banks?.map((b) => b.map(mapItem)),
275	      armor: player.armor?.map(mapItem),
276	      dye: player.dye?.map(mapItem),
277	      trash: mapItem(player.trash ?? null),
278	      miscEquips: player.miscEquips?.map(mapItem),
279	      miscDyes: player.miscDyes?.map(mapItem),
280	      loadouts: player.loadouts?.map((l) => ({
281	        armor: l.armor.map(mapItem),
282	        dye: l.dye.map(mapItem),
283	      })),
284	    },
285	    npcs: townNpcs ?? [],   // 城镇 NPC 段(WorldFile.cs:1703-1750 NPC 段;曾硬编码 [] 断链)
286	    signs: meta.signs,
287	    // 挂物族：存放物 id 走与 chests 相同的稳定 id 映射（mapItem 同构，保留 prefix）
288	    furniture: meta.furniture?.map((e) => ({
289	      x: e.x, y: e.y, kind: e.kind,
290	      slots: e.slots.map((s) => (s ? { id: s.id === 0 ? 0 : ITEM_STABLE_OF_INTERNAL[s.id] ?? 0, stack: s.stack, prefix: s.prefix } : null)),
291	    })),
292	    flags: meta.flags,
293	    weather: meta.weather,
294	    events,
295	    bestiary: meta.bestiary,
296	    idScheme: 'sheet',
297	  };
298	  return JSON.stringify(data);
299	}
300	

```

20	function b64ToBytes(b64: string): Uint8Array {
21	  const s = atob(b64);
22	  const u8 = new Uint8Array(s.length);
23	  for (let i = 0; i < s.length; i++) u8[i] = s.charCodeAt(i);
24	  return u8;
25	}
26	
27	function readVarint(u8: Uint8Array, pos: { i: number }): number {
28	  let v = 0, shift = 0;
29	  while (pos.i < u8.length) {
30	    const b = u8[pos.i++];
31	    v |= (b & 0x7f) << shift;
32	    if (!(b & 0x80)) break;
33	    shift += 7;
34	  }
35	  return v;
36	}

134	  const v4 = (data.version ?? 1) >= 4;

136	    it && v3 ? { ...it, id: it.id === 0 ? 0 : ITEM_INTERNAL_OF_STABLE.get(it.id) ?? 0 } : it;
137	  const mapLegacySlot = (it: SavedItemT | null): SavedItemT | null => {
138	    if (!it) return it;

142	    const key = LEGACY_KEY_OF_INTERNAL_V3[it.id];

185	  if (data.header.dungeonX !== undefined) world.dungeonX = data.header.dungeonX;

187	  if (data.header.dungeonY !== undefined && data.header.dungeonY > 0) world.dungeonY = data.header.dungeonY;

189	  if (data.header.jungleX !== undefined) world.jungleX = data.header.jungleX;
190	  world.clock.timeOfDay = data.clock.timeOfDay;

193	  if (data.clock.bloodMoon !== undefined) world.clock.bloodMoon = data.clock.bloodMoon;
194	  if (data.clock.eclipse !== undefined) world.clock.eclipse = data.clock.eclipse;
195	  if (data.clock.moonPhase !== undefined) world.clock.moonPhase = data.clock.moonPhase;
196	  if (data.header.moonType !== undefined) world.moonType = data.header.moonType;
197	  if (data.header.seedFlags) world.seedFlags = { ...data.header.seedFlags };
198	  // 世界难度（Main.GameMode，Main.cs:2677）——旧档缺省 0=经典
199	  if (data.header.difficulty !== undefined) world.difficulty = data.header.difficulty;
200	  // 旅程时间倍率/冻结（CreativePowers.cs:866/:1448）——旧档缺省 1/false
201	  if (data.header.journeyTimeRate !== undefined) world.journeyTimeRate = data.header.journeyTimeRate;
202	  if (data.header.journeyFreezeTime !== undefined) world.journeyFreezeTime = data.header.journeyFreezeTime;
203	  if (data.invasion) { // 入侵五元组回填（旧档缺省=无入侵）

205	    world.invasionSize = data.invasion.size;
206	    world.invasionSizeStart = data.invasion.sizeStart;
207	    world.invasionX = data.invasion.x;

213	  if (data.weather) world.applyWeather(data.weather); // 天气回填（旧档缺省=晴）
214	  world.flags = data.flags;

225	  );

227	  const st = world.store;
228	  // tiles(v3:RLE 里的 type 是稳定 id,查 INTERNAL_OF_STABLE 还原;v2 旧档=内部下标直读)
229	  {
230	    const v3 = (data.version ?? 1) >= 3;
231	    const u8 = b64ToBytes(data.tiles);
232	    const pos = { i: 0 };
233	    let i = 0;
234	    while (i < st.type.length && pos.i < u8.length) {
235	      const run = readVarint(u8, pos) + 1;
236	      const tRaw = readVarint(u8, pos);
237	      const t = v3 ? (tRaw === 0 ? 0 : TILE_INTERNAL_OF_STABLE[tRaw]) : tRaw;
238	      let fx = 0, fy = 0;
239	      if (t !== 0) {
240	        fx = readVarint(u8, pos);
241	        fy = readVarint(u8, pos);
242	      }
243	      for (let k = 0; k < run && i < st.type.length; k++, i++) {
244	        st.type[i] = t; st.flags[i] = t !== 0 ? 1 : 0;
245	        st.frameX[i] = fx; st.frameY[i] = fy;
246	      }
247	    }
248	  }
249	  // walls（version<2 的旧档墙值迁移：1=土→2, 2=石→1, 3=木→4（现为原版 id））
250	  {
251	    const legacy = (data.version ?? 1) < 2;
252	    const migrate = (v: number) => (legacy ? (v === 1 ? 2 : v === 2 ? 1 : v === 3 ? 4 : v) : v);
253	    const u8 = b64ToBytes(data.walls);
254	    const pos = { i: 0 };
255	    let i = 0;
256	    while (i < st.wall.length && pos.i < u8.length) {
257	      const run = readVarint(u8, pos) + 1;
258	      const v = migrate(readVarint(u8, pos));
259	      for (let k = 0; k < run && i < st.wall.length; k++, i++) st.wall[i] = v;
260	    }
261	  }
262	  // wire 电路位（旧存档缺此字段 → 全 0 = 无导线，缺字段容忍同 liquidType）
263	  {
264	    const u8 = b64ToBytes(data.wire ?? '');
265	    const pos = { i: 0 };
266	    let i = 0;
267	    while (i < st.wire.length && pos.i < u8.length) {
268	      const run = readVarint(u8, pos) + 1;
269	      const v = readVarint(u8, pos);
270	      for (let k = 0; k < run && i < st.wire.length; k++, i++) st.wire[i] = v;
271	    }
272	  }
273	  // half/slope(旧存档缺 → 全整砖;v3+ 语义)
274	  {
275	    const u8 = b64ToBytes(data.blocks ?? '');
276	    const pos = { i: 0 };
277	    let i = 0;
278	    while (i < st.half.length && pos.i < u8.length) {
279	      const run = readVarint(u8, pos) + 1;
280	      const v = readVarint(u8, pos);
281	      for (let k = 0; k < run && i < st.half.length; k++, i++) {
282	        st.half[i] = v === 1 ? 1 : 0;
283	        st.slope[i] = v >= 2 && v <= 5 ? v - 1 : 0;
284	      }
285	    }
286	  }
287	  // liquid type（旧存档缺此字段 → 全 0，由液体模拟按 1=水 补全）
288	  {
289	    const u8 = b64ToBytes(data.liquidType ?? '');
290	    const pos = { i: 0 };
291	    let i = 0;
292	    while (i < st.liquidType.length && pos.i < u8.length) {
293	      const run = readVarint(u8, pos) + 1;
294	      const v = readVarint(u8, pos);
295	      for (let k = 0; k < run && i < st.liquidType.length; k++, i++) st.liquidType[i] = v;
296	    }
297	  }
298	  // liquid
299	  {
300	    const u8 = b64ToBytes(data.liquid);
301	    const pos = { i: 0 };
302	    let i = 0;
303	    while (i < st.liquid.length && pos.i < u8.length) {
304	      const run = readVarint(u8, pos) + 1;
305	      const v = readVarint(u8, pos);
306	      for (let k = 0; k < run && i < st.liquid.length; k++, i++) st.liquid[i] = v;
307	    }
308	  }
309	  return { world, player: data.player, data };
310	}

```
using System;
using Microsoft.Xna.Framework;
using Terraria.ID;

namespace Terraria.GameContent;

public struct PlayerSleepingHelper
{
	public const int BedSleepingMaxDistance = 96;

	public const int TimeToFullyFallAsleep = 120;

	public bool isSleeping;

	public int sleepingIndex;

	public int timeSleeping;

	public Vector2 visualOffsetOfBedBase;

	public bool FullyFallenAsleep
	{
		get
		{
			if (isSleeping)
			{
				return timeSleeping >= 120;
			}
			return false;
		}
	}

	public void GetSleepingOffsetInfo(Player player, out Vector2 posOffset)
	{
		if (isSleeping)
		{
			posOffset = visualOffsetOfBedBase * player.Directions + new Vector2(0f, (float)sleepingIndex * player.gravDir * -4f);
		}
		else
		{
			posOffset = Vector2.Zero;
		}
	}

	private bool DoesPlayerHaveReasonToActUpInBed(Player player)
	{
		if (NPC.AnyDanger(quickBossNPCCheck: true))
		{

/** 入侵进行中（SpawnHomelessNPC/SpawnTravelNPC 压制门，WorldGen.cs:4987） */
export function invasionActive(w: World): boolean {
  return w.invasionType > 0 && w.invasionDelay === 0 && w.invasionSize > 0;
}

/** CanStartInvasion（Main.cs:63863-63881）：无进行中 + 冷却为 0（ignoreDelay 可跳）+ 生命上限≥200。
 *  单人 n=1；规模公式（StartInvasion :63904-63913）按合格玩家人数，单人固定档 */
export function canStartInvasion(w: World, playerMaxHp: number, ignoreDelay = false): boolean {
  if (w.invasionType !== 0) return false;
  if (w.invasionDelay !== 0 && !ignoreDelay) return false;
  return playerMaxHp >= 200;
}

/** StartInvasion（Main.cs:63884-63933）。返回公告用的 misc 编号（approaching west/east）
 *  供调用方 newText——文案本身无参数，方向由 invasionX 决定 */
export function startInvasion(w: World, type: number): number | null {
  if (w.invasionType !== 0 && w.invasionSize === 0) w.invasionType = INVASION_NONE; // 允许胜利后立刻开
  if (w.invasionType !== 0) return null;
  const n = 1; // 单人
  w.invasionSize = 80 + 40 * n;
  if (type === INVASION_PIRATE) w.invasionSize += 40 + 20 * n;   // 海盗 120+60n
  if (type === INVASION_MARTIAN) w.invasionSize = 160 + 40 * n;  // 火星 160+40n（:63898-63900）
  w.invasionSizeStart = w.invasionSize;
  // invasionX（:63920-63932）：非火星 50/50 世界左右边缘；与 spawnX 的相对方向决定公告文案
  w.invasionX = Math.random() < 0.5 ? 0 : w.store.w;
  // 火星前线直接压在出生点旁一格（:63922-63925 invasionX=spawnTileX-1）——下一帧

## 🤖 Assistant · 2026-08-12T17:27:45.875Z · glm-x-preview-260804

<details open>
<summary>💭 thinking</summary>

````
OK. For the dawn goblin roll, the vanilla-1:1 approach: roll first (when shadowOrbSmashed), then on hit call `startInvasion(w, INVASION_GOBLIN)` directly (which internally gates `invasionType !== 0` and requires the player count with maxHp>=200 — for us single player maxHp>=200). But `canStartInvasion` includes the delay gate which vanilla StartInvasion doesn't. Hmm: vanilla StartInvasion: `if (invasionType != 0 && invasionSize == 0) invasionType = 0; if (invasionType != 0) return;` — then requires `num > 0` (players with maxHp>=200). No delay gate. So for dawn rolls, call `startInvasion` directly (not via startInvasionAndAnnounce's canStartInvasion which would toast messages — vanilla dawn rolls are silent if they fail).

So I'll inline: 
```ts
if (w.flags.shadowOrbSmashed) {
  const hit = w.flags['downedGoblins']
    ? (w.flags.hardMode ? Math.random() < 1 / 60 : Math.random() < 1 / 30)
    : Math.random() < 1 / 3;
  if (hit) { const misc = startInvasion(w, INVASION_GOBLIN); if (misc != null && misc >= 0) { this.invasionWarn = 3600; this.newText(Lang.misc(misc), 175, 75, 255); } }
}
```
Hmm, but that duplicates announce logic. Maybe better to keep `startInvasionAndAnnounce` but that has the toast + delay gate. Let me restructure minimally: keep the roll gated only on `w.flags.shadowOrbSmashed` (removing invasionType/canStartInvasion from the goblin condition), then on hit call `startInvasionAndAnnounce(INVASION_GOBLIN, true)` (ignoreDelay=true to skip the delay gate — vanilla has no delay gate here). The toast on failure... `canStartInvasion` fails only if invasionType!=0 (→ toast "BossActive" — wrong message for dawn) or maxHp<200 (toast Need200Hp). Hmm, toasts during a natural roll would be wrong. Vanilla is silent.

Actually — is there another existing path for natural invasion roll announcements? Let me search for how snow legion/martian natural rolls announce. There's a martian natural roll somewhere (line 7666: "自然触发走海盗 roll（hardMode + altarCount>0）"). Let me look at 7660-7690 to see the pattern used there.
````

118	                   //   （Player.cs:10000-10004 + :25616；满 debuff -25% 线性恢复。
119	                   //   ★33 是 Weak(近战-5.1%伤/速) 勿混）
120	  Obstructed = 68, // 遮挡(163)：headcovered + bleed（:11421-11425）——bleed 清自然恢复
121	                   //   计时（:18998 同流血 30）；headcovered → ScreenObstruction 遮屏
122	                   //   （目标 0.95/步进 0.3，ScreenObstruction.cs:27-31）。
123	                   //   由星云头蟹 421 头顶吸附每 tick AddBuff(163,59)（NPC.cs:39199）
124	}

213	/** Main.buffNoSave 全名单（Main.cs:8896-8990：不写入玩家存档的 buff;
214	 *  含 173-181 整段 for 循环）——本仓仅存 BUFF_DEFS 已映射项,交集生效 */
215	const BUFF_NO_SAVE = new Set([
216	  20, 22, 23, 24, 28, 29, 30, 31, 34, 35, 37, 38, 39, 43, 44, 46, 47, 48,
217	  58, 59, 60, 62, 63, 64, 67, 68, 69, 70, 72, 80, 87, 88, 89, 93, 94, 95,
218	  96, 97, 98, 99, 100, 103, 119, 120, 125, 126, 133, 134, 135, 137, 139,
219	  140, 144, 146, 147, 150, 158, 159, 161, 163, 164, 170, 171, 172, 173, 174,
220	  175, 176, 177, 178, 179, 180, 181, 182, 187, 188, 194, 195, 196, 197, 198,
221	  199, 205, 213, 214, 215, 263, 271, 320, 321, 322, 325, 335, 348, 353, 355,
222	  366, 385, 386,
223	]);
224	/** vanilla buff id → 本仓 BuffType 反查（存档恢复用） */
225	const BUFF_TYPE_BY_VANILLA = new Map<number, BuffType>(
226	  (Object.keys(BUFF_DEFS) as unknown as BuffType[]).map((t) => [BUFF_DEFS[t].vanillaBuff, t]),
227	);
228	
229	/** Buff 名:原版 BuffName.<Internal>(Lang 未就绪回退中文兜底) */
230	export function buffName(t: BuffType): string {
231	  return Lang.buffName(BUFF_DEFS[t].vanillaBuff) ?? BUFF_DEFS[t].fallbackName;
232	}
233	
234	/** Buff 描述:自有键(机制数值是本仓库实现,不用原版通用描述) */
235	export function buffDesc(t: BuffType): string {
236	  return Lang.text(BUFF_DEFS[t].descKey);
237	}
238	
239	/** HP/s 汇总表（UpdateBuffs lifeRegen 项换算：lifeRegen N = N/2 HP/s） */
240	const HP_PER_SEC: Partial<Record<BuffType, number>> = {
241	  [BuffType.Regen]: 2,   // lifeRegen+4
242	  [BuffType.Honey]: 1,   // lifeRegen+2
243	};
244	
245	/** DoT 分块大小（Player.cs:19136-19204 结算 + HurtLifeRegen:19207）：burned/
246	 *  suffocating 走 -600 阈值分支 → 每 600 life-regen 点弹 HurtLifeRegen(5)；
247	 *  其余（OnFire 等）走通用 -120 分支 → 每 120 点弹 1。换算到时间：
248	 *  Burning(−60/t)=每 10t 弹 5（≈3 次/秒飘字）、Suffocation(−40/t)=每 15t 弹 5、
249	 *  OnFire(−8/t)=每 15t 弹 1。掉血须整块弹出并伴随 LifeRegen 色(255,60,70)
250	 *  dot 飘字（CombatText.cs:24 / NewText dot:true），非每帧静默 1 HP 滴流 */
251	const DOT_CHUNK: Partial<Record<BuffType, number>> = {
252	  [BuffType.Burning]: 5,
253	  [BuffType.Suffocation]: 5,
254	};
255	
256	export class BuffState {
257	  active = new Map<BuffType, number>(); // 剩余秒（≈buffTime/60）
258	  campfireAccum = 0;
259	  lanternAccum = 0;
260	  /** 当前篝火光环强度(Game 每 20tick 扫描写入:篝火+1,心灯额外+1) */
261	  campfireHeal = 1;
262	  /** 心灯(lifeRegen+2 = 1 HP/s,Player.cs:12247):Game 扫描写入 0/1 */
263	  lanternHeal = 0;
264	  /** AddBuff 语义（Player.cs:5138-5150）：同类取 max 不缩短——浸入类 buff 可每帧无脑续期 */
265	  apply(type: BuffType, durationSec: number) {
266	    const cur = this.active.get(type);
267	    if (cur === undefined || cur < durationSec) this.active.set(type, durationSec);
268	  }
269	  has(type: BuffType): boolean {
270	    return this.active.has(type);
271	  }
272	  remove(type: BuffType) {
273	    this.active.delete(type);
274	  }
275	  /** 直写剩余秒（原版 `buffTime[i] = n` 赋值语义，可缩短——区别于 apply 的
276	   *  AddBuff max 合并）。消费方：玩家微光穿落 buffTime=6t 刷新（Player.cs:11416） */
277	  set(type: BuffType, durationSec: number) {
278	    this.active.set(type, durationSec);
279	  }
280	  /** 存档序列化（Player.cs:53913-53925 buffType+buffTime 槽位语义）:
281	   *  存 vanilla id + 剩余秒;Main.buffNoSave 名单(Main.cs:8896-8990)过滤不落盘的 buff */
282	  toSave(): Array<[number, number]> {
283	    const out: Array<[number, number]> = [];
284	    for (const [t, sec] of this.active) {
285	      const v = BUFF_DEFS[t]?.vanillaBuff;
286	      if (v === undefined || BUFF_NO_SAVE.has(v)) continue;
287	      out.push([v, Math.max(1, Math.round(sec))]);
288	    }
289	    return out;
290	  }
291	  /** 读档恢复（旧档/导入缺省=无 buff;未知 vanilla id 静默丢弃——后续接入的 buff 不丢档） */
292	  applySaved(list: Array<[number, number]> | undefined): void {
293	    if (!list) return;
294	    for (const [v, sec] of list) {
295	      if (BUFF_NO_SAVE.has(v)) continue;
296	      const t = BUFF_TYPE_BY_VANILLA.get(v);
297	      if (t !== undefined) this.active.set(t, sec);
298	    }
299	  }
300	  /** 本帧弹出的 DoT 分块伤害合计（HurtLifeRegen 语义,Player 消费后弹出飘字并清零） */
301	  private dotChunkOut = 0;
302	  consumeDotChunk(): number {
303	    const c = this.dotChunkOut;
304	    this.dotChunkOut = 0;
305	    return c;
306	  }
307	  /** 每逻辑帧（dt 秒）。返回本帧生命净变化（正=回复,负=持续伤害/DoT,无视无敌帧与防御） */
308	  tick(dt: number): number {
309	    let heal = 0;
310	    for (const [t, time] of [...this.active]) {
311	      const left = time - dt;
312	      if (left <= 0) this.active.delete(t);
313	      else this.active.set(t, left);
314	    }
315	    // 定额 HP/s buff（Regeneration 2 HP/s / Honey 1 HP/s）
316	    heal += this.rateAccum(BuffType.Regen, 2, dt);
317	    heal += this.rateAccum(BuffType.Honey, 1, dt);
318	    // 环境 DoT debuff（Player.cs:18793-18934 lifeRegen 负项 / 2 = HP/s）——
319	    // 分块弹出（DOT_CHUNK），净伤害记入 dotChunkOut 供 HurtLifeRegen 飘字消费
320	    const dots =
321	      this.rateAccum(BuffType.OnFire, -4, dt)         // lifeRegen-8
322	      + this.rateAccum(BuffType.Burning, -30, dt)     // lifeRegen-60
323	      + this.rateAccum(BuffType.Suffocation, -20, dt); // lifeRegen-40
324	    heal += dots;
325	    if (dots < 0) this.dotChunkOut += -dots;
326	    // 蜂蜜对冲（Player.cs:18934-18940:lifeRegen<0 时 honey 额外 lifeRegen+4 = +2 HP/s）——
327	    // DoT 系统已实装,补上 buff-system-port 备忘里标"暂缺"的分支
328	    const dotActive = heal < 0;
329	    if (dotActive && this.active.has(BuffType.Honey)) {
330	      heal += this.rateAccum(BuffType.Honey, 2, dt, true);
331	    }
332	    if (this.active.has(BuffType.Werewolf)) {
333	      // 狼人 lifeRegen++（:11496）= 0.5 HP/s → 每 2 秒 1 HP
334	      this.campfireAccum += dt;
335	      if (this.campfireAccum >= 2) { this.campfireAccum -= 2; heal += this.campfireHeal; }
336	    }
337	    if (this.active.has(BuffType.Campfire)) {
338	      // 原版 lifeRegen+1 = 0.5 HP/s(Player.cs:18990/12244-12245)→ 每 2 秒 1 HP
339	      this.campfireAccum += dt;
340	      if (this.campfireAccum >= 2) {
341	        this.campfireAccum -= 2;
342	        heal += this.campfireHeal;
343	      }
344	    }
345	    if (this.lanternHeal > 0) {
346	      this.lanternAccum += dt;
347	      if (this.lanternAccum >= 1) {
348	        this.lanternAccum -= 1;
349	        heal += this.lanternHeal;
350	      }
351	    }
352	    return heal;
353	  }
354	  /** 定额回复/伤害 buff 的独立累加器（Map 存每 buff 的秒余数;负速率=DoT）。
355	   *  separate=true 用独立累加器键(蜂蜜对冲与常规蜂蜜回复分账,避免共用余数)。
356	   *  负速率按 DOT_CHUNK 整块弹出（≈原版 lifeRegenCount 阈值分块）。已知偏差:
357	   *  原版 lifeRegenCount 是全局共享计数器,buff 到期后负余量仍残留(由后续
358	   *  DoT/自然回复对冲);本实现 per-buff 累加器到期即弃余数(<1 块,≤4 HP,一次性) */
359	  private rateLeft = new Map<string, number>();
360	  private rateAccum(t: BuffType, hpPerSec: number, dt: number, separate = false): number {
361	    if (!this.active.has(t)) { this.rateLeft.delete(`${t}:${separate}`); return 0; }
362	    const key = `${t}:${separate}`;
363	    let left = (this.rateLeft.get(key) ?? 0) + hpPerSec * dt;
364	    let heal = 0;
365	    if (hpPerSec >= 0) {
366	      while (left >= 1) { left -= 1; heal += 1; }
367	    } else {
368	      const chunk = DOT_CHUNK[t] ?? 1;
369	      // 原版 lifeRegenCount 是整数累加（-600/-120 阈值精确）;此处 dt 浮点积分

The thorns reflect in Game.ts (~8889): `attacker.hurt(reflect, Math.sign(attacker.cx - p.cx) || 1, -1.5, this)` — kb -1.5 upward, direction = sign(attacker.cx - p.cx). Vanilla: `ApplyDamageToNPC(npc, num5, knockback=10, -num3, false)` where num3 = 1 if npc center X < player center X else -1; direction passed = -num3 → if npc is left of player, num3=1, direction=-1 (push left, away). Our `Math.sign(attacker.cx - p.cx)` = +1 if attacker right of player → pushes right (away). Correct direction sign, but kb -1.5 vs 10, and it's applied via `hurt` which presumably gives vertical knock too? The finding says "(现上抛)" — currently tosses upward. So the hurt call's kb=-1.5 gives upward launch. Vanilla: ApplyDamageToNPC with direction -num3 → purely horizontal knockback (velocity.X). So fix: pass kb=10 and horizontal-only.

Let me look at `attacker.hurt` signature and the kb semantics, plus how the direction argument is used. Let me read Enemy.hurt.

Also finding 12: thorns triggered by projectiles. Need to see the callers of this damagePlayer code — the `attacker` param. Let me find the function signature containing line 8889.

So vanilla StrikeNPC knockback (normal branch, gravity NPC): `velocity.Y = -num3 * 0.75 * knockBackResist; velocity.X = num3 * hitDirection * knockBackResist`. Hmm — there IS a vertical component (-num3*0.75*kbResist). So "纯水平" from the finding... Let me re-read: the knockback from thorns uses `ApplyDamageToNPC(npc, num5, knockback=10, -num3)` — that's the standard StrikeNPC path with kb=10 and direction = -num3 (horizontal, away from player). The velocity is standard kb physics: X = num3*dir*kbResist, Y = -num3*0.75*kbResist (a hop). So not literally "pure horizontal" — but the *direction argument* is purely horizontal (no upward launch bias beyond standard kb).

The key point vs our implementation: ours does `attacker.hurt(reflect, Math.sign(attacker.cx - p.cx) || 1, -1.5, this)` — kbx=±1, kby=-1.5 fixed values, NOT scaled by kb=10. Vanilla: velocity.X = 10*kbResist*dir, velocity.Y = -7.5*kbResist. So our current is a fixed ±1/-1.5 kick.

How does our Enemy.hurt map kbx/kby? `this.vx += kbx * (1 - resist); this.vy += kby * (1 - resist);`. Hmm — that's an *additive delta* model, quite different from vanilla's *set* model. So to emulate vanilla kb=10 for thorns I need to produce kbx/kby deltas equal to vanilla's target velocity change. But since our hurt adds deltas rather than setting, and callers elsewhere pass small values... Let me check how normal melee hits compute kbx/kby to understand the scale used in this codebase (e.g. player weapon hits pass kb values like?). Let me grep for `.hurt(` calls with arguments to see the convention.

`liquidCollision(st, px, py, pw, ph, liquidType)` — box scan with `liquid > 0 && liquidType === type`. That's exactly the WetCollision-style box scan for honey. So for the honey detection: `liquidCollision(world.store, this.x, this.y, this.w, this.h, 3)` (full body box, liquid > 0).

Careful with the accumulator: rateAccum returns integer chunks; clamping is fine.

Now writing the new tick() DoT structure:

```ts
tick(dt: number): number {
  let heal = 0;
  for (const [t, time] of [...this.active]) { ...decay... }
  // ---- UpdateBuffs 正项（Player.cs:9640+ Regeneration lifeRegen+=4 / Honey :18955 lifeRegen+=2）----
  heal += this.rateAccum(BuffType.Regen, 2, dt);
  heal += this.rateAccum(BuffType.Honey, 1, dt);
  // ---- DoT 段（NaturalLifeRegen :18779-18911）：每个 DoT 先 if(lifeRegen>0) lifeRegen=0
  //      再扣——恢复类 buff 不能抵消 OnFire/Burning（曾简单相加，恢复池被 DoT 吃掉）----
  let dots = 0;
  for (const [t, hp] of DOTS) {  // OnFire -4 / Burning -30 / Suffocation -20 (HP/s)
    if (!this.active.has(t)) continue;
    if (heal > 0) heal = 0;                    // :18781 等 if (lifeRegen > 0) lifeRegen = 0
    const d = this.rateAccum(t, hp, dt);
    dots += d; heal += d;
  }
  if (dots < 0) this.dotChunkOut += -dots;
  // 蜂蜜对冲（:18915-18922：lifeRegen<0 时 +4 且结果钳回 ≤0）
  if (heal < 0 && this.active.has(BuffType.Honey)) {
    heal += this.rateAccum(BuffType.Honey, 2, dt, true);
    if (heal > 0) heal = 0;
  }
  ...
}
```

where DOTS is a const list: `[[BuffType.OnFire, -4], [BuffType.Burning, -30], [BuffType.Suffocation, -20]]`.

```ts
/** 鹿角怪自然生成（NPC.SpawnOnPlayer 语义：直接落在玩家身上） */
private spawnDeerclopsOnPlayer(): void {
  const p = this.player;
  const e = Enemy.fromVanilla(668, p.cx, p.cy);
  if (!e) return;
  e.x = p.cx - e.w / 2;
  e.y = p.cy - e.h / 2;
  e.id = this.entities.nextId++;
  this.entities.add(e, 'enemies');
  if (!this.boss) this.boss = e;
}
```

 "new_string": "  /** 旅程模式时间冻结（CreativePowers.FreezeTime，CreativePowers.cs:1448——开启时 dayRate=0） */\n  journeyFreezeTime = false;\n  /** 附魔日晷/月晷快进（Main.fastForwardTimeToDawn/Dusk + sundialCooldown/moondialCooldown，\n   *  Main.cs:6240-6268 Sundialing/Moondialing：使用置旗 + 冷却 8 天；快进中 dayRate=60。\n   *  清旗点 = UpdateTime_StartDay :64844-64847（dawn）/ StartNight :64691-64694（dusk），\n   *  冷却递减 = StartDay :64885-64887（sundial）/ StartNight :64695-64697（moondial），\n   *  血月清零 = StartNight :64892-64893。随世界存档持久化（WorldFile.cs:1311/1342/1416-1417） */\n  fastForwardTimeToDawn = false;\n  fastForwardTimeToDusk = false;\n  sundialCooldown = 0;\n  moondialCooldown = 0;\n  /** 全员熟睡旗（CurrentFrameFlags.SleepingPlayersCount==ActivePlayersCount && >0，\n   *  Main.cs:6280-6285——Game 每 tick 下发；单人 = 本地玩家 sleeping && timeSleeping>=120） */\n  allSleeping = false;\n  /** IsFastForwardingTime（Main.cs:6230-6238）——事件 roll 压制门共用 */\n  isFastForwardingTime(): boolean { return this.fastForwardTimeToDawn || this.fastForwardTimeToDusk; }\n  /** 专家世界（Main.cs:2775-2777：GameMode==1/2 → Difficulty≥Expert；旅程 3 不算） */\n  get isExpert(): boolean { return this.difficulty === 1 || this.difficulty === 2; }\n  /** 大师世界（Main.cs:2779-2780：仅 GameMode==2） */\n  get isMaster(): boolean { return this.difficulty === 2; }\n  /** 旅程世界（Main.cs:2696 IsJourneyMode：GameMode==3） */\n  get isJourney(): boolean { return this.difficulty === 3; }\n  /** 当前 dayRate（Main.cs:6270-6294 UpdateTimeRate 1:1）：快进恒 60；\n   *  否则冻结 0 / TargetTimeRate × 全员睡 5。时钟/天气/税计时/旅行商概率全链消费 */\n  dayRate(): number {\n    if (this.isFastForwardingTime()) return 60;\n    if (this.journeyFreezeTime) return 0;\n    return this.journeyTimeRate * (this.allSleeping ? 5 : 1);\n  }\n  /** 时间推进倍率（Main.cs:6278-6290 UpdateTimeRate：冻结 0 / 否则 TargetTimeRate；gameMenu 恒 1）\n   *  ——不含快进/睡觉两档的旧入口，保留给不参与 dayRate 链的调用方 */\n  journeyTimeScale(): number { return this.journeyFreezeTime ? 0 : this.journeyTimeRate; }"
}

360	  private rateAccum(t: BuffType, hpPerSec: number, dt: number, separate = false): number {
361	    if (!this.active.has(t)) { this.rateLeft.delete(`${t}:${separate}`); return 0; }
362	    const key = `${t}:${separate}`;
363	    let left = (this.rateLeft.get(key) ?? 0) + hpPerSec * dt;
364	    let heal = 0;
365	    if (hpPerSec >= 0) {
366	      while (left >= 1) { left -= 1; heal += 1; }
367	    } else {
368	      const chunk = DOT_CHUNK[t] ?? 1;
369	      // 原版 lifeRegenCount 是整数累加（-600/-120 阈值精确）;此处 dt 浮点积分
370	      // 在块边界会差 ~1e-15（如 1/3×15=4.999…）,补 epsilon 保证阈值时点对齐
371	      while (left <= -chunk + 1e-9) { left += chunk; heal -= chunk; }
372	    }
373	    this.rateLeft.set(key, left);
374	    return heal;

 "new_string": "  /** 每逻辑帧（dt 秒）。返回本帧生命净变化（正=回复,负=持续伤害/DoT,无视无敌帧与防御） */\n  tick(dt: number): number {\n    let heal = 0;\n    for (const [t, time] of [...this.active]) {\n      const left = time - dt;\n      if (left <= 0) this.active.delete(t);\n      else this.active.set(t, left);\n    }\n    // ---- UpdateBuffs 正项（Player.cs:9640+）：Regeneration lifeRegen+=4（2 HP/s）、\n    //      Honey :18955 lifeRegen+=2（1 HP/s）、狼人 :11496 lifeRegen+=1（0.5 HP/s）。\n    //      注意原版这些都在 NaturalLifeRegen 的 DoT 段（:18779+）**之前**求值——\n    //      DoT 的 if(lifeRegen>0) lifeRegen=0 会把它们整池清零（见下）----\n    heal += this.rateAccum(BuffType.Regen, 2, dt);\n    heal += this.rateAccum(BuffType.Honey, 1, dt);\n    if (this.active.has(BuffType.Werewolf)) {\n      // 狼人 lifeRegen++（:11496）= 0.5 HP/s → 每 2 秒恒 1 HP（独立累加器，勿与篝火\n      // 共用 campfireAccum/campfireHeal——曾误挂致篝火不在时狼人回复随强度归零/错峰）\n      this.werewolfAccum += dt;\n      if (this.werewolfAccum >= 2) { this.werewolfAccum -= 2; heal += 1; }\n    }\n    // ---- DoT 段（NaturalLifeRegen :18779-18911）：每个 DoT 先 if(lifeRegen>0)\n    //      lifeRegen=0 再累加负项——恢复类 buff **不能**抵消 OnFire/Burning 等\n    //      （曾直接相加，恢复池被 DoT 按净额吃掉）。分块弹出（DOT_CHUNK），\n    //      净伤害记入 dotChunkOut 供 HurtLifeRegen 飘字消费 ----\n    let dots = 0;\n    for (const [t, hpPerSec] of ENV_DOTS) {\n      if (!this.active.has(t)) continue;\n      if (heal > 0) heal = 0;                       // :18781 等 if (lifeRegen > 0) lifeRegen = 0\n      const d = this.rateAccum(t, hpPerSec, dt);\n      dots += d;\n      heal += d;\n    }\n    if (dots < 0) this.dotChunkOut += -dots;\n    // 蜂蜜对冲（Player.cs:18915-18922：lifeRegen<0 时 honey 额外 lifeRegen+4 = +2 HP/s，\n    // 且对冲后钳回 ≤0）——DoT 系统已实装,补上 buff-system-port 备忘里标\"暂缺\"的分支\n    if (heal < 0 && this.active.has(BuffType.Honey)) {\n      heal += this.rateAccum(BuffType.Honey, 2, dt, true);\n      if (heal > 0) heal = 0;\n    }\n    // ---- 光环正项（NaturalLifeRegen :18988-18993，位于 DoT 段之后——篝火/心灯\n    //      的 lifeRegen 按原版可对冲 DoT）----\n    if (this.active.has(BuffType.Campfire)) {\n      // 原版 lifeRegen+1 = 0.5 HP/s(Player.cs:18990/12244-12245)→ 每 2 秒 1 HP\n      this.campfireAccum += dt;\n      if (this.campfireAccum >= 2) {\n        this.campfireAccum -= 2;\n        heal += this.campfireHeal;\n      }\n    }\n    if (this.lanternHeal > 0) {\n      this.lanternAccum += dt;\n      if (this.lanternAccum >= 1) {\n        this.lanternAccum -= 1;\n        heal += this.lanternHeal;\n      }\n    }\n    return heal;\n  }"
}

For the stopEvents computation I need a helper since it's used in 3 places (dawn, dusk rolls, night spawn gate). Let me add a private method:

```ts
/** ShouldNormalEventsBeAbleToStart（Main.cs:64652-64660）：灯笼夜 OR
 *  天界入侵进行中 OR 场上有月总 OR 月总倒计时>0 → 压制普通事件 roll */
private stopEvents(): boolean {
  const w = this.world;
  return LanternNight.lanternsUp || w.lunarEvent.active
    || w.lunarEvent.moonLordCountdown > 0
    || this.entities.enemies.some((e) => { const en = e as Enemy; return !en.dead && en.vanillaId === 398; });
}
```

Wait, but at dawn the stopEvents must be evaluated BEFORE checkMorning (which may clear lanternsUp) — calling this.stopEvents() before LanternNight.checkMorning() ✓.

Note the moonLord check via entities — NPC.AnyNPCs(398). ✓

Then dawn block: `const stopEvents = this.stopEvents();` replacing lanternStopEvents, and use it in the eclipse/invasion rolls.

**Edit B**: Dusk block — moondial fast-forward clear + moondialCooldown-- (vanilla StartNight head). Then the rolls gate: replace `!LanternNight.lanternsUp` with `!this.stopEvents()` — but careful: stopEvents must be evaluated AFTER LanternNight.checkNight() (StartNight: LanternsUp is set inside StartNight via `if (LanternNight.LanternsUp) stopEvents = true;` — after CheckNight ran at :64709). Our existing code evaluates `!LanternNight.lanternsUp` in the EoC/mech/blood-moon rolls which come after checkNight in the same dusk-crossing block ✓. So replace with `!stopEventsNow` computed after checkNight.

Also add `!w.isFastForwardingTime()` to the EoC/mech roll block and the blood moon roll (vanilla gates the whole StartNight roll section with `!IsFastForwardingTime() && !stopEvents`).

Also blood moon → clear sundial/moondial cooldowns (vanilla :64892-64893).

**Edit C**: Night spawn block (EoC/mech/deerclops): add `!w.isFastForwardingTime() && !this.stopEvents()` gate (vanilla :64542 `if (!IsFastForwardingTime() && !stopEvents)`), and add the deerclops midnight crossing.

**Edit D**: taxTimer += dayRate.

**Edit E**: travel merchant (threshold 0.9, dayRate probability, fast-forward gate).

Let me start with Edit A. The dawn block begins around line 2832. Let me view the opening.
````

 "new_string": "  private spawnMoonLordOnPlayer(): void {\n    if (this.boss) return;   // 已有 Boss 在场（原版 SpawnOnPlayer 同样照召；此处避免双 Boss 槽冲突）\n    const p = this.player;\n    const e = Enemy.fromVanilla(398, p.cx, p.cy - 160);\n    if (!e) return;\n    e.x = p.cx - e.w / 2;\n    e.y = p.cy - 160 - e.h / 2;\n    e.id = this.entities.nextId++;\n    this.entities.add(e, 'enemies');\n    this.boss = e;\n  }\n\n  /** 鹿角怪(668)雨天自然生成（NPC.SpawnOnPlayer 语义：直接落在玩家身上，\n   *  Main.cs:64612——午夜 16200t + 下雨 + 雪原无城镇等门全过后调用） */\n  private spawnDeerclopsOnPlayer(): void {\n    if (this.boss) return;\n    const p = this.player;\n    const e = Enemy.fromVanilla(668, p.cx, p.cy);\n    if (!e) return;\n    e.x = p.cx - e.w / 2;\n    e.y = p.cy - e.h / 2;\n    e.id = this.entities.nextId++;\n    this.entities.add(e, 'enemies');\n    this.boss = e;\n  }"
}

    // 判定区比视觉小一圈(与渲染 scale 不一致的"盒贴图不匹配"来源)
    const vScale = v.scale ?? 1;
    e.w = Math.round(v.width * vScale);
    e.h = Math.round(v.height * vScale);
    e.spawnAlpha = v.alpha ?? 0; // 原版 SetDefaults alpha（静态不透明度，NPC.Opacity=1-alpha/255）
    // EoW 族 alpha=255 = 出生全透明渐显标记（其余 alpha 为静态不透明度,勿动）：
    // 钳到 254 并置 alphaFade,由 fixedUpdate 逐 tick 减回 0（原版 AI_006 渐显）
    if (e.spawnAlpha >= 255) { e.spawnAlpha = 254; e.alphaFade = true; }
    e.colorRGBA = v.color ? [v.color[0], v.color[1], v.color[2], v.color[3] ?? 255] : null; // 原版 color 字段
    // 266 克脑 SetDefaults :12372 dontTakeDamage=true——一阶段（爬行者存活）不可击，
    // AI_054 二阶段入场段 :32589 自行解除（存档载入即二阶段也由该段下一 tick 清除）
    if (id === 266) e.dontTakeDamage = true;
    e.x = x - e.w / 2;
    e.y = y - e.h / 2;
    return e;
  }

[高] 睡眠 ×5 — 已修，按原版语义（与任务提示有一处出入，注明）** `dayRate() = journeyTimeRate × (全员熟睡 ? 5 : 1)`，时钟与天气两条消费链都从 `journeyTimeScale()` 切到 `dayRate()`。`timeSleeping` 在 fixedUpdate 递增（含 DoesPlayerHaveReasonToActUpInBed 的血月夜/日食昼/危险/用物重置），`allSleeping = sleeping && timeSleeping>=120`（FullyFallenAsleep）。**出入**：任务提示"城镇 NPC+玩家全睡"，但原版 Main.cs:6280+17706-17708 只数玩家（`sleeping.FullyFallenAsleep`）——按"反编译源码是唯一标杆"取原版，单人即本地玩家熟睡。

**9. [高] 狼人回复 — 已修**：独立 `werewolfAccum`、恒 1HP/2s，不再共用 `campfireAccum/campfireHeal`；并按原版顺序移到 DoT 段之前（会被 DoT 清零）；`!merman` 门加在授予点（Player.ts:1479，原版 else→DelBuff，人鱼化/白天现在会移除 buff）。
